                                                                                    Annual Report 2021/2022 年度報告   5
    CHAIRMAN’S
    STATEMENT
    主席報告




       Since 1940, the year of Vitasoy’s founding, we have been
       providing consumers with delicious and nutritional plant-
       based food and beverages. Although FY2021/2022 was
       particularly challenging for us, we are confident in our core
       strengths and ability to gradually bring our business back to
       a path of sustainable and profitable growth.
       維他奶自一九四零年創立以來，一直秉承為消費者提供以植物成
       分為主，既美味又營養豐富的食品及飲品的宗旨。儘管二零二一
       ╱二零二二財政年度充滿挑戰，但憑藉我們的核心優勢以及雄厚
       實力，我們堅信我們的業務將可逐步重拾可持續盈利增長。




               Mr. Winston Yau-lai LO
               (SBS, BSc, MSc)
               羅友禮先生 (SBS, BSc, MSc)

               Executive Chairman
               執行主席

                                                                         Annual Report 2021/2022 年度報告   9
     GROUP CHIEF
     EXECUTIVE OFFICER’S
     REPORT/BUSINESS REVIEW
     集團行政總裁報告╱業務回顧




        FY2021/2022 was a challenging year, owing to the continued
        impact of the COVID-19 pandemic that was further
        aggravated by the underperformance of our Mainland
        China business. We are confident that by focusing on the
        execution of core portfolio and strategic innovation we will
        be able to gradually restore our growth trajectory.
        受 2019 冠狀病毒病疫情持續影響，加上本集團中國內地業務表
        現未如理想，二零二一╱二零二二財政年度甚具挑戰。我們深
        信，憑藉本集團持續專注於核心產品組合及策略創新，我們將逐
        步重回增長軌道。




                Mr. Roberto GUIDETTI
                陸博濤先生

                Executive Director and
                Group Chief Executive Officer
                執行董事暨集團行政總裁

disruptions in both supply chains and consumption patterns.         鏈及消費端受到衝擊。此外，我們最大市場，中

market operating units such as the Hong Kong SAR, Australia         14%。
and New Zealand, and Singapore.

weaker sales in Mainland China, profits were also affected by       銷售疲弱影響外，公司溢利亦因有關 2019 冠狀
the reduction in COVID-19 subsidies that had been received          病毒病政府補貼較去年第一階段減少影響。該

both the sales execution of our core business and innovation,       管中國內地於二零二二年四月至六月季度內出現
and our go-to-market strategy. Although Mainland China              2019 冠狀病毒病疫情，待疫情情況緩和及經濟環
experienced COVID-19 in the quarter from April to June 2022,        境改善後，我們將準備就緒加快各項目的進度。
we will be ready to accelerate our programmes’ impact when
the pandemic situation subsides and economy improves.

To counter the impact of rising material costs, we have             面對材料成本上漲，我們繼續於採購合約中預先
continued to anticipatively take selective pricing actions          採取特選定價措施及鎖定原材料價格，同時嚴格
and lock in raw material prices in our procurement contracts,       控制經營費用及資本支出。
whilst exercising strict controls on operating expenses and
capital expenditure.

our efforts to bring new products to market that appeal to          品以吸引不同客戶群，如下文營運單位的特定分

Health and Safety during COVID-19                                        於 2019 冠狀病毒病疫情期間的健康
                                                                         與安全
The health and safety of our staff and partners has been a               本集團於疫情期間一直高度重視員工及合作夥伴
top priority of the Group during the pandemic. We continued              的健康與安全。我們持續全面遵守及符合所有政
to comply fully with all government regulations and stepped              府法規，以及加強實施現有的預防措施。該等措
up our existing precautionary measures. These included                   施包括辦公室和廠房嚴格執行訪客控制措施、員
strict visitor controls in our offices and plant premises, Rapid-        工上班前須進行快速抗原檢測，及安排辦公室的
Antigen Tests for staff before reporting for duty, and work-             僱員在家工作。我們亦提供疫苗假期、為須接受
from-home arrangements for office-based employees. We                    強制隔離或檢測規定的僱員提供有薪休假、員工
also provided vaccination leave, paid time-off for compulsory            上班下班的額外穿梭巴士，以及定期健康講座。
quarantine or testing requirements, and additional shuttle
buses for staff commuting to work, as well as regular health
talks.

     Financial Risk Management                                        財務風險管理
     The Group’s overall financial management policy focuses on       本集團的整體財務管理政策強調預測及管控風
     anticipating, controlling and managing risks, and covering       險，涵蓋與本集團的相關業務直接有關的交易。
     transactions directly related to the underlying businesses       為達致協同效益、效率及監控的目的，本集團為
     of the Group. For synergy, efficiency and control, the Group     所有附屬公司實行中央現金及財政管理系統。各
     operates a central cash and treasury management system           營運附屬公司一般以當地貨幣進行借貸，為當地

     Company to the Executive Directors and Senior Management            管治守則或其他規管要求規定，部分特定事項不

     Board Independence                                                     董事會獨立性
     The Company recognises that Board independence is pivotal              本公司深知董事會獨立性對良好企業管治及董事
     in good corporate governance and board effectiveness. The              會效能至關重要。董事會相信，本公司已設立有
     Board believes that the Company has effective mechanisms in            效機制可確保本公司任何董事的獨立觀點及意見
     place to ensure independent views and input from any director          能夠傳達予董事會，以增加決策的客觀性和成效
     of the Company are conveyed to the Board for enhancing an              性。董事會通過其薪酬及提名委員會定期審查管
     objective and effective decision making. The governance                治框架及機制，以確保其成效。薪酬及提名委員
     framework and mechanisms are under regular review by the               會於二零二二年六月就該等機制進行以下檢討：
     Board, through its Remuneration and Nomination Committee
     (the “R & N Committee”), to ensure their effectiveness. The R &
     N Committee had performed the review of such mechanisms
     in June 2022 as follows:


      Key Features or Mechanisms                                  Implementation and Review of Effectiveness
      主要特點或機制                                                     執行及檢討成效

      Number of Independent Non-executive Directors               Five out of the eleven directors are INEDs which is near
      (INEDs)                                                     to half of the total Board members and also exceeds
      獨立非執行董事人數                                                   the Listing Rules requirement for INEDs to make up at
                                                                  least one third of the Board.
                                                                  十一名董事中有五名為獨立非執行董事，接近董事會成員
                                                                  總數的一半，亦超過上市規則規定獨立非執行董事佔董事
                                                                  會人數最少三分之一的要求。

                                                                  Nearly half of the Board members are INEDs which
                                                                  ensure that independent views could be heard and be
                                                                  thoroughly considered during the Board meetings and
                                                                  decision making by the Board.
                                                                  近半數董事會成員為獨立非執行董事，確保在董事會會議
                                                                  及董事會決策過程中能夠聽取及充分考慮獨立意見。

                                                                  Chairpersons of major Board Committees are INEDs.
                                                                  主要董事委員會主席均為獨立非執行董事。



      The R & N Committee to assess and confirm independence of   The R & N Committee assesses the independence of
      INEDs                                                       a candidate for a new INED appointment and also
      薪酬及提名委員會評估及確認獨立非執行董事的獨立性                                    the continued independence of existing INEDs on
                                                                  an annual basis. All INEDs are required to confirm in
                                                                  writing annually their compliance of independence
                                                                  requirements as set out under Rule 3.13 of the Listing
                                                                  Rules.
                                                                  薪酬及提名委員會評估新任獨立非執行董事候選人的獨立
                                                                  性，以及每年評估現有獨立非執行董事的持續獨立性。所
                                                                  有獨立非執行董事須每年書面確認其遵守上市規則第3.13
                                                                  條所載的獨立性規定。

                                                                  Through the review by the R & N Committee and the
                                                                  self-declaration of each INED, the Board could ensure
                                                                  the independence of each INED.
                                                                  透過薪酬及提名委員會的審核及各獨立非執行董事的自我
                                                                  聲明，董事會可確保各獨立非執行董事的獨立性。

Director Nomination Policy                           The Policy sets out the selection and nomination criteria
董事提名政策                                               and procedures for appointment/re-appointment
                                                     of Directors. Independence is one of the factors to
                                                     be considered for appointment/re-appointment of
                                                     Directors, in particular for INED appointment.
                                                     該政策載有委任╱重新委任董事的甄選及提名標準以及程
                                                     序。獨立性是考慮委任╱重新委任董事（尤其是獨立非執
                                                     行董事）的因素之一。

                                                     The R & N Committee would take account of the
                                                     independence factor in accordance with the Policy
                                                     before making recommendations to the Board for
                                                     approval of the appointment/re-appointment of
                                                     INED.
                                                     薪酬及提名委員會向董事會提交批准委任╱重新委任獨立
                                                     非執行董事的建議前，根據該政策考慮獨立性因素。



Directors Conflict of Interest Policy                The Company has policy and procedures in place to
董事利益衝突政策                                             avoid any potential conflict of interests and not to
                                                     weaken the objectivity and integrity of the Board for
                                                     decisions making. Under the Policy, any Director has
                                                     to declare his/her directorships outside the Company,
                                                     interests in controlling company, public office
                                                     appointment and any other interests that might be
                                                     considered prejudicial to his/her independence and
                                                     has to declare his/her interests in any transaction in
                                                     which he/she may have interests and abstain from
                                                     voting on such transaction.
                                                     本公司已制定政策及程序以避免任何潛在利益衝突，且不
                                                     會削弱董事會決策的客觀性及誠信性。根據該政策，任何
                                                     一位董事必須申報其於本公司以外的董事職務、於控股公
                                                     司中的權益、公職任命及任何其他可能被認為有礙其獨立
                                                     性的利益，並須申報其於可能擁有利益的任何交易中的權
                                                     益，並就該交易放棄投票。

      Open Board Culture                               INEDs in family businesses will generally play a more
      開明的董事會文化                                         active role in corporate governance area. Our Board
                                                       culture under the leadership of Executive Chairman,
                                                       encourages questions and challenges from Directors
                                                       in particular from INEDs who may hold different views
                                                       from the Executive Directors and Senior Management
                                                       and their comments and concerns will be closely
                                                       followed up and addressed by the Executive Directors
                                                       and Senior Management.
                                                       家族企業中的獨立非執行董事一般會於公司管治方面發揮
                                                       更為積極的作用。在執行主席領導下，我們的董事會文
                                                       化鼓勵董事（尤其是獨立非執行董事）提出問題及質疑，
                                                       彼等可能與執行董事及高層管理人員持有不同觀點，而執
                                                       行董事及高層管理人員將密切跟進及解決彼等的意見及關
                                                       注。



      No equity-based remuneration with performance-   Directors fees payable to Directors, including INEDs,
      related elements to INEDs                        are fixed fees without any discretionary element nor
      獨立非執行董事未曾授予與表現績效相關的股本權益酬金                        they are remunerated with equity-based awards.
                                                       Directors Fees are also determined by the R & N
                                                       Committee and the Board with reference to the roles
                                                       and responsibilities taken up by the Director and
                                                       benchmarking against peers.
                                                       應付董事（包括獨立非執行董事）的董事袍金為固定數
                                                       額，並無任何酌情因素，而酬金亦無包括以股權為基礎的
                                                       獎勵。董事袍金亦由薪酬及提名委員會與董事會參照董事
                                                       所承擔的角色及職責以及行業標準而釐定。



      Access to independent professional advice        External independent professional advice is available
      獲得獨立專業建議                                         to all Directors, including INEDs, whenever deemed
                                                       necessary.
                                                       如有需要，所有董事（包括獨立非執行董事）均可獲得外
                                                       部獨立專業意見。

                                                       With prior consultation with the Chairman of the Board,
                                                       Directors would be reimbursed for any professional
                                                       fees in relation to their fulfilment of the roles and
                                                       responsibilities.
                                                       在事先與董事會主席諮詢後，董事可報銷與其履行職務及
                                                       責任有關的任何專業費用。

Board Proceedings                                                           董事會程序
The Board met five times in FY2021/2022 to discuss and                      董事會於二零二一╱二零二二財政年度內召開五
approve the Group’s long term strategic plan; to review and                 次會議，討論及批准本集團之長期策略規劃；審
monitor the financial and business performance of the Group                 視及監察本集團及各營運單位之財務及業務表
and each of its operating units; to consider and approve                    現；考慮及審批年度預算及財務報告；審閱及批
annual budget, the financial reports; to review and approve                 准可持續發展報告及與企業管治及可持續發展相
sustainability report and various corporate governance and                  關之各種政策及常規；並監察及審視本集團風險
sustainability related policies and practices; and to monitor               管理表現之成效。
and review the effectiveness of the Group’s risk management
performance.

The Board and its Committees are supplied with full and                     董事會及其委員會均適時獲得本公司所有相關資
timely information in relation to the Company, which enables                料，以履行職責。本公司已有既定程序致使董事
them to discharge their responsibilities. There are established             在適當的情況下，為履行其職責及責任而諮詢獨
procedures for Directors to seek independent professional                   立專業意見，費用由本公司支付。
advice for them to discharge their duties and responsibilities,
where appropriate, at the Company’s expenses.

Directors have made active participation in the Board                       年內，董事均積極參與董事會會議、董事委員會
meetings, Board Committees meetings and the Annual                          會議及股東週年大會。

Director Nomination Policy                                         董事提名政策
The Board has adopted a Director Nomination Policy in March        董事會已於二零一二年三月採納董事提名政策，
2012 to set out the procedures and criteria for identifying and    當中載列物色及揀選準候選人擔任新任董事以及
selecting potential candidates for the appointment of new          考慮續展現任董事委任之程序及準則。薪酬及提
Director/s as well as for considering the renewal of director      名委員會已獲董事會授權以物色具備合適資格成
appointment. The Board has delegated to the Remuneration           為董事會成員的人士，並揀選或向董事會推薦有
and Nomination Committee the authority to identify                 關被提名人士出任董事；審視獨立非執行董事的

his responsibilities as the Chairman of the Board. Board

     Risk Management and Internal Control                               風險管理及內部監控
     The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk             董事會明白其肩負風險管理及內部監控系統之責
     management and internal control systems and reviewing              任，並檢討其成效。該系統之設計旨在管理而非
     their effectiveness. The systems are designed to manage            消除無法達成業務目標之風險，並僅可為避免發
     rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business      生重大錯誤陳述或損失提供合理而非絕對之保
     objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not absolute       證。為實現此目標，董事會確保已制定有效之風
     assurance against material misstatement or loss. To achieve        險管治架構及持續風險管理程序，以達致本公司
     this, the Board ensures that there is an effective framework       業務之長久成功。
     of risk governance and ongoing risk management process to
     promote the long-term success of the Company.

     ongoing basis for ensuring that appropriate and effective          理及內部監控之整體職責，從而提供合理保證，

     Risk Management Process                                             風險管理程序
     Our risk management process is embedded in our daily                我們之風險管理程序已納入日常營運範圍、財務
     operations, financial activities, compliance, strategy              活動、合規、策略發展與業務規劃、投資決定及
     development & business planning, investment decision and            外界因素，程序如下：
     external factors. The processes are as follows:

     •    Anticipate: Potential risks and threats that are related       •   預測： 所有員工均參與識別不同業務流程
          to different business processes and relevant to the                及與本公司目標相關之潛在風險及威脅。
          Company’s objectives will be anticipated and identified            為便於進行適當評估，我們將風險分為六
          by all individual staff. Risks are grouped into six clusters       個範疇（包括信譽、策略、市場、流動資
          (including reputation, strategy, market, liquidity,                金、信貸及營運）。
          credit and operation) to facilitate the assessment
          appropriately.

     •    Rate: Each identified risk is evaluated on the basis of        •   評估： 按照董事會設定之風險承受能力，
          impact and likelihood consistent with the risk appetite            評估每項經識別風險的影響及可能性。風
          set by the Board. The risk impact and likelihood will              險之影響及可能性將根據過往記錄、統計
          be rated according to the historical records, statistical          分析、相關經驗及管理層之專業判斷進行
          analyses and relevant experiences, specialist and expert           評級。集團風險管理促進評估流程，包括
          judgments by management. Group Risk Management                     協助企業風險管理執行委員會及審核委員
          facilitates the evaluation process including the summary           會之討論。
          discussion in the Enterprise Risk Management Executive
          Committee and Audit Committee.

     •    Respond: A delegated management is assigned to                 •   控制： 各項風險將配對專責管理人員，成
          each risk as risk owner to fully respond on each risk.             為風險負責人，以有效管理風險。風險負
          Risk management strategies and corresponding control               責人制定風險管理策略以及指派監控負責
          owners are identified by risk owner to deploy appropriate          人，實施適當監控活動。
          control activities.

     •    Regulate: We take a close monitoring on the current            •   監察： 我們密切監控風險現況。各風險現
          status of risk, which is being monitored through key risk          況的結果取決於關鍵風險指標的預設量度
          indicators with defined thresholds, occurrence of related          級別基準、所發生之相關事故、內部監控
          incidents, control effectiveness and audit results. Any            程序之成效以及審計結果。風險現況倘出
          change in current status of risk to a worsened level will          現任何不良轉變，均會啟動反應程序，展
          trigger a react process through creation of a mitigation           開減輕風險計劃。
          plan.

     •    React: When there is a change on the current status of         •   緩解： 當風險現況有所改變，相應負責人
          risk, the corresponding owner will coordinate mitigation           將協調減輕風險計劃之行動細節、負責人
          plan with action details, responsible person and timeline          及時間表，以處理風險情況。
          to address the status.

     the overriding principle that inside information should be         資料，除非有關資料屬於《證券及期貨條例》中

     information comes to our attention unless it falls within any      管體系下有關內幕消息披露規定，本公司已有一
     of the safe harbours provisions under the Securities and           套擁有既定政策及程序之系統。董事會已於二零
     Futures Ordinance. The Company has had a system in place           一二年十一月二十七日採納披露合規政策，並於

     with the Inside Information disclosure requirements under          修訂。該政策乃參照證券及期貨事務監察委員會
     the regulatory regime. The Board has adopted a Disclosure          所頒佈之《內幕消息披露指引》而編製，旨在為

     to the “Guidelines on Disclosure of Inside Information” issued     或使用公司之機密資料以及內幕消息。
     by the Securities and Futures Commission. The purpose of the
     Policy is to provide Directors and employees with guidelines

Dividend Policy                                                     股息政策
The Board has adopted a Dividend Policy to provide guidance         董事會已採納股息政策，就派發盈餘資金予股東
to the Board and the management of the Company for                  向董事會及管理層提供指引。
distribution of surplus funds to its shareholders.

In determining the level of dividend payment, the following         就決定所派付的股息水平，須計及下列準則及考
criteria and consideration must be taken:                           慮因素：

–    Sharing of success with shareholders                           –   與股東共享成果
–    Liquidity and capital requirement                              –   流動資金及資本要求
–    Historical trend of dividend payout                            –   過往股息派付之趨勢
–    Benchmarking with competitors within the industry              –   與業內競爭對手之標準比較
–    Fulfilment of financial covenants                              –   符合財務契約
–    Taxation impact                                                –   稅務影響
–    Restrictions                                                   –   限制條件
–    Other factors that may deem relevant                           –   任何其他視為相關之因素

The Company intends to declare dividends to shareholders to         本公司計劃配合扣除一次性事件影響後之股東應
align with the fluctuation in profit attributable to shareholders   佔溢利之波動向股東宣派股息，並可能除中期或
net of one-off event impact and may declare special dividends       末期股息外不時宣派特別股息。董事會將不時酌
from time to time in addition to interim or final dividends. The    情決定是否派發股息以及所派發股息之水平。
determination to pay dividends and level of dividend to be
distributed will be subject to the discretion of the Board from
time to time.

The Company adopted a share award scheme on 22nd March             本公司已於二零二一年三月二十二日採納一項股
2021 (the “Share Award Scheme”) under which the Directors          份獎勵計劃（「股份獎勵計劃」），據此，董事可

an award of restricted shares, performance shares, restricted      制股份單位或表現股份單位之獎勵。股份獎勵
share units or performance share units. The purpose of the         計劃旨在吸引及挽留管理層和主要僱員、使合資
Share Award Scheme is to attract and retain management             格參與者的利益與本公司的長遠成功保持一致、
and key employees, to align eligible participants’ interests       為管理層和主要僱員提供公平和具有競爭力的薪
with the long-term success of the Company, to provide fair         酬，並推動實現本公司的策略目標。
and competitive compensation to management and key
employees and to drive the achievement of the Company’s
strategic objectives.

by the Board and is administered by the Board and the trustee      事會及股份獎勵計劃受託人管理。根據股份獎勵
of the Share Award Scheme. The total number of shares to           計劃授予的股份總數不得超過本公司不時已發行

Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of                          核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的責
the consolidated financial statements                                任
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about              我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在
whether the consolidated financial statements as a whole             由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理

 (a) Statement of compliance                                           (a) 遵例聲明
 These financial statements have been prepared in accordance            本財務報表是按照所有適用之《香港財務報告準
 with all applicable Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards            則》、香港公認會計原則及香港《公司條例》之規
 (“HKFRSs”), which collective term includes all applicable              定而編製。《香港財務報告準則》一詞包括香港
 individual Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards, Hong               會計師公會頒佈之所有適用之個別《香港財務報
 Kong Accounting Standards (“HKASs”) and Interpretations                告準則》、《香港會計準則》及詮釋。本財務報表
 issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public                  亦符合香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則
 Accountants (“HKICPA”), accounting principles generally               （「上市規則」）之適用披露規定。本集團採納之
 accepted in Hong Kong and the requirements of the Hong                 主要會計政策於下文披露。
 Kong Companies Ordinance. These financial statements also
 comply with the applicable disclosure provisions of the Rules
 Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of
 Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing rules”). Significant accounting
 policies adopted by the Group are disclosed below.

 The HKICPA has issued certain amendments to HKFRSs that               香港會計師公會已頒佈若干《香港財務報告準則》
 are first effective or available for early adoption for the current   之修訂本，並於本集團及本公司之當前會計期間
 accounting period of the Group and the Company. Note 1(c)             首次生效或可供提早採納。首次應用此等適用於
 provides information on any changes in accounting policies            本集團之新訂準則所引致於當前及過往會計期間
 resulting from initial application of these developments to           之任何會計政策變動已於本財務報表內反映，有
 the extent that they are relevant to the Group for the current        關資料載於附註 1(c)。
 and prior accounting periods reflected in these financial
 statements.


 (b) Basis of preparation of the financial statements                  (b) 財務報表之編製基準
 The consolidated financial statements for the year ended              截至二零二二年三月三十一日止年度之綜合財務
 31st March 2022 comprise the Company and its subsidiaries             報表包括本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）
 (together referred to as the “Group”) and the Group’s interest        以及本集團於一間合營公司之權益。
 in a joint venture.

 The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on           儘管本集團於報告期末有凈流動負債
 a going concern basis notwithstanding that the Group had net          475,424,000 元，綜合財務報表依然按持續經
 current liabilities of $475,424,000 at the end of the reporting       營基準編製。於編製本綜合財務報表時，董事已
 period. In preparing these consolidated financial statements,         審慎考慮本集團目前及預期未來流動資金。經考
 the Directors have given careful consideration to the current         慮（其中包括）(i) 於二零二二年三月三十一日之現
 and anticipated future liquidity of the Group. Taking into            金及銀行存款 621,863,000 元，(ii) 於報告期末
 account, inter alia, (i) cash and bank deposits of $621,863,000       尚未使用之信貸額，及 (iii) 本集團於未來十二個
 at 31st March 2022, (ii) the unutilised loan facilities at the        月營運所產生之預期現金流入淨額，董事認為，
 end of the reporting period, and (iii) the expected net cash          本集團將能應付其到期負債。因此，董事認為按
 inflows generated from the Group’s operations for the next            持續經營基準編製本綜合財務報表乃屬恰當。
 twelve months, the Directors are of the opinion that the Group
 will be able to meet its liabilities as and when they fall due.
 Accordingly, the Directors consider that the preparation of
 these consolidated financial statements on a going concern
 basis is appropriate.

       and future periods.

 discussed below:                                                      下：


 Amendment to HKFRS 16, Covid-19-related rent                          《香港財務報告準則》第 16 號之修訂「於二零
 concessions beyond 30 June 2021 (2021 amendment)                      二一年六月三十日後之 2019 冠狀病毒病相
                                                                       關租金寬減」
                                                                            （二零二一年修訂）
 The Group previously applied the practical expedient in               本集團先前應用《香港財務報告準則》第 16 號中
 HKFRS 16 such that, as lessee, it was not required to assess          之可行權宜方法，故作為承租人，倘符合資格條
 whether rent concessions occurring as a direct consequence            件，則毋需評估直接因 2019 冠狀病毒病疫情而
 of the COVID-19 pandemic were lease modifications, if the             產生之租金寬減是否為租賃調整（見附註 1(j)）。
 eligibility conditions were met (see note 1(j)). One of these         其中一項條件要求租賃付款之減少僅影響於指定
 conditions requires the reduction in lease payments affect            時限內或之前到期之原始付款。二零二一年修
 only payments originally due on or before a specified time            訂將該時限由二零二一年六月三十日延長至二零
 limit. The 2021 amendment extends this time limit from 30th           二二年六月三十日。
 June 2021 to 30th June 2022.

 The Group has adopted the 2021 amendment in this financial            本集團已於本財政年度採納二零二一年修訂。該
 year. There is no impact on the opening balance of equity at          修訂對二零二一年四月一日之年初權益結餘並無
 1st April 2021.                                                       影響。


 Amendments to HKFRS 9, HKAS 39, HKFRS 7, HKFRS                        《香港財務報告準則》第 9 號、《香港會計準
 4 and HKFRS 16, Interest rate benchmark reform –                       則》第 39 號、《香港財務報告準則》第 7 號、
 phase 2                                                               《香港財務報告準則》第 4 號及《香港財務報
                                                                        告準則》第 16 號之修訂「利率基準改革 － 第
                                                                       2 階段」
 The amendments provide targeted relief from (i) accounting            該等修訂提供有關下列方面的針對情況作出會計
 for changes in the basis for determining contractual cash flows       緩衝安排：(i) 將釐定金融資產、金融負債及租賃
 of financial assets, financial liabilities and lease liabilities as   負債之合約現金流量之基準變動作為調整進行會
 modifications, and (ii) discontinuing hedge accounting when           計處理；及 (ii) 由於銀行同業拆息改革，當利率
 an interest rate benchmark is replaced by an alternative              基準被替代基準利率取代時，則終止對沖會計處
 benchmark rate as a result of the reform of interbank offered         理。該等修訂並無對本財務報表造成影響，因本
 rates (“IBOR reform”). The amendments do not have an                  集團並無與基準利率掛鈎且受銀行同業拆息改革
 impact on these financial statements as the Group does not            影響之合約。
 have contracts that are indexed to benchmark interest rates
 which are subject to the IBOR reform.

       (d) Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                     (d) 附屬公司及非控股權益
       Subsidiaries (including structured entities) are entities          附屬公司（包括結構性實體）為本集團所控制之
       controlled by the Group. The Group controls an entity when         實體。當本集團從參與某實體之業務獲得或有權
       it is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its         獲得可變回報，及有能力藉對實體行使其權力而
       involvement with the entity and has the ability to affect those    影響該等回報，則本集團控制該實體。當評估本
       returns through its power over the entity. When assessing          集團是否有權力時，只考慮具體權利（由本集團
       whether the Group has power, only substantive rights (held by      及其他人士持有）。
       the Group and other parties) are considered.

       A structured entity is an entity that has been designed so that    結構性實體則指那些在釐定誰是其控制人時、投
       voting or similar rights are not the dominant factor in deciding   票權或類似權利並非重要考量的實體，例如當任
       who controls the entity, such as when any voting rights relate     何投票權僅與行政工作有關，而且相關的業務活
       to administrative tasks only and the relevant activities are       動是透過合約安排方式指導。
       directed by means of contractual arrangements.

       An investment in a subsidiary is consolidated into the             於附屬公司之投資由該控制權生效日期起至結束
       consolidated financial statements from the date that control       日期止期間於綜合財務報表綜合入賬。集團內公
       commences until the date that control ceases. Intra-group          司之間之結餘、交易及現金流量，以及集團內公
       balances, transactions and cash flows and any unrealised           司之間之交易所產生之任何未變現溢利，均於編
       profits arising from intra-group transactions are eliminated       製綜合財務報表時全數撇銷。如無減值證據，集
       in full in preparing the consolidated financial statements.        團內公司之間之交易產生之未變現虧損按未變現
       Unrealised losses resulting from intra-group transactions are      收益相同之方式撇銷。
       eliminated in the same way as unrealised gains but only to
       the extent that there is no evidence of impairment.

       Non-controlling interests represent the equity in a subsidiary     非控股權益指非本公司直接或間接應佔之附屬公
       not attributable directly or indirectly to the Company, and        司股權，而本集團並未與有關權益持有人協定任
       in respect of which the Group has not agreed any additional        何附加條款，令本集團整體對該等權益產生符合
       terms with the holders of those interests which would result       金融負債定義之合約義務。就各企業合併而言，
       in the Group as a whole having a contractual obligation            本集團可選擇按公允值或非控股權益所佔附屬公
       in respect of those interests that meets the definition of a       司之可識別資產淨值之比例計量任何非控股權
       financial liability. For each business combination, the Group      益。
       can elect to measure any non-controlling interests either at
       fair value or at the non-controlling interests’ proportionate
       share of the subsidiary’s net identifiable assets.

       Non-controlling interests are presented in the consolidated        非控股權益在綜合財務狀況表之權益部份內，與
       statement of financial position within equity, separately          本公司股權持有人應佔權益分開呈列。非控股權
       from equity attributable to the equity shareholders of the         益所佔本集團業績之權益在綜合損益表及綜合損
       Company. Non-controlling interests in the results of the Group     益及其他全面收益表呈列，以顯示本年度之總溢
       are presented on the face of the consolidated statement            利或虧損及全面收益總額於非控股權益與本公司
       of profit or loss and the consolidated statement of profit or      股權持有人之間之分配。非控股權益持有人之貸
       loss and other comprehensive income as an allocation of            款及該等持有人之其他合約責任乃按該筆負債之
       the total profit or loss and total comprehensive income for        性質根據附註 1(n) 或 (o) 在綜合財務狀況表呈列為
       the year between non-controlling interests and the equity          金融負債。
       shareholders of the Company. Loans from holders of non-
       controlling interests and other contractual obligations
       towards these holders are presented as financial liabilities in
       the consolidated statement of financial position in accordance
       with notes 1(n) or (o) depending on the nature of the liability.

        and liabilities measured as at the acquisition date.                負債之公允值淨額之部份。

       At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether the          於合約開始時，本集團會評估合約是否屬租賃或

       consideration. Control is conveyed where the customer has           用，亦有權自該使用中獲得絕大部分經濟利益，
       both the right to direct the use of the identified asset and to     則控制權已轉移。

       use.

 The Group recognises a loss allowance for expected credit          本集團為按攤銷成本計量之金融資產（包括現金

 receivables and loan to joint venture).                            撥備。

 Measurement of ECLs                                                預期信貸虧損之計量
 ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit losses.         預期信貸虧損乃信貸虧損之概率加權估計。信貸
 Credit losses are measured as the present value of all             虧損以所有預期現金差額（即根據合約應付予本
 expected cash shortfalls (i.e. the difference between the cash     集團之現金流量與本集團預期收取之現金流量之
 flows due to the Group in accordance with the contract and         間的差額）的現值計量。
 the cash flows that the Group expects to receive).

 Where the effect of discounting is material, the expected          倘折現之影響屬重大，則預期現金差額將採用應
 cash shortfalls are discounted using the effective interest rate   收賬款及其他應收款初始確認時釐定之實際利率
 determined at initial recognition or an approximation thereof      或其近似值折現。
 for trade and other receivables.

 The maximum period considered when estimating ECLs is the          估計預期信貸虧損時所考慮之最長期間為本集團
 maximum contractual period over which the Group is exposed         面臨信貸風險之最長合約期間。
 to credit risk.

 In measuring ECLs, the Group takes into account reasonable         在計量預期信貸虧損時，本集團考慮合理及有理
 and supportable information that is available without              據而無需付出過多的成本或努力獲得之資料。這
 undue cost or effort. This includes information about past         包括有關過往事件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀況預
 events, current conditions and forecasts of future economic        測的資料。
 conditions.

       (iii) Interim financial reporting and impairment                       (iii) 中期財務報告及減值
       Under the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the              根據香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）證券
       Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”),             上市規則，本集團須按《香港會計準則》第 34 號
       the Group is required to prepare an interim financial report           「中期財務報告 」就財政年度首六個月編製中期財
       in compliance with HKAS 34, Interim financial reporting, in             務報告。本集團於中期期末應用之減值測試、確
       respect of the first six months of the financial year. At the end       認及撥回之準則與於財政年度末所應用之準則相
       of the interim period, the Group applies the same impairment            同（見附註 1(k)(i) 及 (ii)）。
       testing, recognition, and reversal criteria as it would at the
       end of the financial year (see notes 1(k)(i) and (ii)).

       Impairment losses recognised in an interim period in respect           於中期期間就商譽確認之減值虧損不會於隨後期
       of goodwill are not reversed in a subsequent period. This is           間撥回，即使在減值僅於中期期間之有關財政年
       the case even if no loss, or a smaller loss, would have been           度完結時評估之情況下原應確認為零虧損或較少
       recognised had the impairment been assessed only at the end            虧損亦然。
       of the financial year to which the interim period relates.

 (l)   Inventories                                                  (l)   存貨

 value.                                                             賬。

 Cost is calculated using the weighted average method and           成本值乃以加權平均方法計算，並包括所有購貨
 comprises all costs of purchase, costs of conversion and other     成本、加工成本及將存貨運往其現時地點及達至
 costs incurred in bringing the inventories to their present        現有狀態之其他成本。
 location and condition.

 Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in the         可變現淨值指正常業務過程中之估計售價減去完
 ordinary course of business less the estimated costs of            成交易之估計成本及進行銷售所需之估計成本。
 completion and the estimated costs necessary to make the
 sale.

 inventories is recognised as an expense in the period in which     為開支。任何存貨金額撇減至可變現淨值及存貨
 the related revenue is recognised. The amount of any write-        之所有虧損均於撇減或虧損之發生期內確認為開
 down of inventories to net realisable value and all losses of      支。倘存貨之撇減出現任何撥回，則於撥回出現
 inventories are recognised as an expense in the period the         期內將費用作減額確認。
 write-down or loss occurs. The amount of any reversal of any

 in which the reversal occurs.


 (m) Receivables                                                    (m) 應收款
 A receivable is recognised when the Group has an unconditional     本集團具有無條件權利收取代價時確認應收款。
 right to receive consideration. A right to receive consideration   倘代價到期付款前僅需待時間推移，則收取代價
 is unconditional if only the passage of time is required before    之權利為無條件。
 payment of that consideration is due.

 interest method less allowance for credit losses (see note 1(k)    備後入賬（見附註 1(k)(i)）。
 (i)).

 initially recognised and redemption value being recognised in      損益內確認。
 profit or loss over the period of the borrowings, together with

 that asset. Other borrowing costs are expensed in the period       出。

 the contract term. Where practicable, the Group seeks to                項，以提供運營靈活性。本集團於租賃開始日期
 include such extension options exercisable by the Group to              評估是否合理肯定行使延期選項。倘本集團未能

       The Group leases out investment properties under an operating        本集團以經營租賃租出投資物業。該等租賃初步
       lease. The leases runs for an initial period of two years, with an   為期兩年，任何參與方有權提早終止。該等租賃

 The fair value measurement is categorised as a Level 3              公允值計量根據《香港財務報告準則》第 13 號「公
 valuation under the three-level fair value hierarchy as defined     允值計量 」所界定之三級公允值架構分類為第三

      2 inputs i.e. observable inputs which fail to meet                  達到第一級之可觀察輸入數據）且並未使用
      Level 1, and not using significant unobservable inputs.             重大不可觀察輸入數據計量之公允值。不

 –    Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using significant      –    第三級估值：使用重大不可觀察輸入數據

 (a) Impairment                                                      (a) 減值
 During the year ended 31st March 2021, the carrying amount          截至二零二一年三月三十一日止年度，於計及
 of interest in joint venture was fully impaired by recognising      2019 冠狀病毒病對未來銷售預測之不利影響後，
 an impairment charge of $42,800,000 after considering the           通過確認減值費用 42,800,000 元，合營公司之
 adverse impact of COVID-19 on future sales projections.             權益之賬面金額已全數減值。

 The COVID-19 pandemic had resulted in extended periods of           2019 冠狀病毒病疫情已導致封城期延長、人流減
 lockdown, reduced mobility, negatively impacting product            少，對菲律賓的產品需求造成負面影響。2019 冠
 demand in the Philippines. COVID-19 continues to impact the         狀病毒病持續影響菲律賓並對合資公司的前景造
 Philippines and adversely affect the joint venture’s prospects.     成不利影響。管理層認為，2019 冠狀病毒病的影
 Management is of the view that the impact of COVID-19               響將持續一段時間，而這已反映在經修訂的銷售
 will be felt for some time, which is reflected in revised sales     預測中。投資項目的可收回金額根據其使用價值
 projections. The recoverable amount of the investment was           釐定。有關計算使用的現金流量預測是根據管理
 determined based on its value-in-use. The calculation used          層所編製的業務計劃作出。該等業務計劃反映迄
 discounted cash flow projections that are based on business         今為止的最新發展以及對疫情復甦後的未來收益
 plans prepared by management, reflecting the performance            增長的假設。預測期以後的現金流量則使用 2%
 to date and the pandemic recovery assumptions for future            之年增長率推算。
 revenue growth. Cash flows beyond the forecast period are
 extrapolated using an annual growth rate of 2%.

       (g) Capital management                                            (g) 資本管理
       The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital are to       本集團在管理資本時之首要目標為保障本集團能
       safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern,     持續經營的能力，透過將產品及服務的價格訂於
       so that it can continue to provide returns for shareholders       與風險相稱的水平及按合理成本籌措融資，使其
       and benefits for other stakeholders, by pricing products and      能繼續為股東提供回報及為其他利益相關人士提
       services commensurately with the level of risk and by securing    供利益。
       access to finance at a reasonable cost.

       The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its          本集團積極及定期對資本架構進行檢討及管理，
       capital structure to maintain a balance between the higher        以在較高股東回報可能附帶較高借貸水平與雄厚
       shareholder returns that might be possible with higher levels     資本狀況帶來之優勢及保障間取得平衡，並因應
       of borrowings and the advantages and security afforded by a       經濟環境之變化對資本架構作出調整。
       sound capital position, and makes adjustments to the capital
       structure in light of changes in economic conditions.

       Consistent with industry practices, the Group monitors its        本著業內慣例，本集團以負債對經調整資本比率
       capital structure on the basis of a debt-to-adjusted capital      作為監控其資本架構之基準。就此目的，本集團
       ratio. For this purpose, the Group defines debt as the total of   將負債界定為應付票據、銀行貸款及租賃負債之
       bills payable, bank loans and lease liabilities, and adjusted     總額，經調整資本為權益之所有組成部份減非累
       capital as all components of equity less unaccrued proposed       計擬派股息。
       dividends.

       The Group’s strategy, which was unchanged from the year           本集團之策略與截至二零二一年三月三十一日止
       ended 31st March 2021, was to maintain the debt-to-adjusted       年度保持不變，將負債對經調整資本比率維持於
       capital ratio below 30%. In order to maintain or adjust the       30% 以下。為保持或調整有關比率，本集團或會
       ratio, the Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to       調整向股東派付之股息金額、發行新股份、向股
       shareholders, issue new shares, return capital to shareholders,   東退回資本、籌集新債項融資或出售資產以減低
       raise new debt financing or sell assets to reduce debt.           負債。

       (a) Credit risk                                                      (a) 信貸風險

       its contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to the     財務虧損之風險。
       Group.

       The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable to trade and       本集團之信貸風險主要來自應收賬款及其他應收
       other receivables. Management has a credit policy in place           款。管理層備有信貸政策，而所面臨之有關信貸
       and exposure to credit risk is monitored on an ongoing basis.        風險持續受到監控。

       The Group’s cash and bank deposits are placed with financial         本集團之現金及銀行存款乃存放於有良好信貸評
       institutions with sound credit ratings, and the management           級之金融機構，管理層認為本集團面臨之信貸風
       consider the Group’s exposure to credit risk is low.                 險為低。

       The Group does not provide any guarantees which would                本集團並無提供任何將令本集團面臨信貸風險之
       expose the Group to credit risk.                                     擔保。


       Trade debtors and bills receivable (which are included               應收賬款及應收票據（已包括於應收賬款及
       in trade and other receivables)                                      其他應收款內）
       Individual credit evaluations are performed on all customers         所有要求超過特定信貸金額之客戶均須進行個別
       requiring credit over a certain amount. These evaluations            信貸評估。該等評估集中於客戶過往之到期還款
       focus on the customer’s past history of making payments,             記錄及現時還款能力，並會考慮客戶之特定資料
       when due, and current ability to pay, and take into account          以及有關客戶營運所在之經濟環境。應收賬款及
       information specific to the customer as well as pertaining to        應收票據一般於發單日起計一至三個月內到期。
       the economic environment in which the customer operates.             一般而言，本集團不會從客戶取得抵押品。
       Trade debtors and bills receivables are generally due within
       one to three months from the date of billing. Normally, the
       Group does not obtain collateral from customers.

       (“AUD”), Singapore dollars (“SGD”), Philippines Peso (“PHP”),
